# Reference — Gradle and Versions Cheatsheet

Sample version catalog and build files at the November 2026 snapshot
(Kotlin 2.4.21, AGP 9.5, Gradle 9.6, JDK 17, Compose BOM 2026.09.00).

> **Warning: re-check Maven before building.** Versions move. Confirm
> every version below on Google Maven (https://maven.google.com) or
> Maven Central (https://central.sonatype.com) and the Compose BOM
> mapping page before pinning a real project. Items marked
> **Unverified** were not pinned to an exact value in this snapshot.

## `gradle/libs.versions.toml`

```toml
[versions]
kotlin = "2.4.21"
agp = "9.5.0"            # AGP 9.5 line — confirm exact patch on Google Maven
ksp = "2.3.0"            # KSP line matching Kotlin 2.4.x — confirm on Maven Central
composeBom = "2026.09.00"
coreKtx = "1.17.0"       # confirm on Google Maven
lifecycle = "2.9.0"      # confirm on Google Maven
activityCompose = "1.11.0" # confirm on Google Maven
room2 = "2.8.4"          # Room 2 line — OR room3 below, never both
room3 = "3.0.0"          # Room 3 line — confirm current 3.x on Google Maven
navigation = "2.9.0"     # Navigation 2 line — confirm on Google Maven
paging = "3.5.3"
cameraX = "1.6.2"
media3 = "1.11.1"
workManager = "2.11.0"   # confirm on Google Maven
retrofit = "3.0.0"
okhttp = "5.5.1"
ktor = "3.3.0"           # Ktor 3.x line — confirm patch on Maven Central
coroutines = "1.11.0"
serialization = "1.9.0"  # confirm on Maven Central
coil = "3.3.0"           # Coil 3 line — confirm patch on Maven Central
glide = "5.0.0"          # Glide 5 line — confirm on Maven Central
billing = "8.0.0"        # Billing Library 8+ required
gamesV2 = "20.0.0"       # PGS v2 — confirm on Google Maven
# hilt = "..."           # Unverified — read current version at dagger.dev/hilt
# firebaseBom = "..."    # Unverified — read current BoM at firebase.google.com

[libraries]
androidx-core-ktx = { group = "androidx.core", name = "core-ktx", version.ref = "coreKtx" }
androidx-lifecycle-runtime-ktx = { group = "androidx.lifecycle", name = "lifecycle-runtime-ktx", version.ref = "lifecycle" }
androidx-lifecycle-viewmodel-compose = { group = "androidx.lifecycle", name = "lifecycle-viewmodel-compose", version.ref = "lifecycle" }
androidx-activity-compose = { group = "androidx.activity", name = "activity-compose", version.ref = "activityCompose" }
androidx-compose-bom = { group = "androidx.compose", name = "compose-bom", version.ref = "composeBom" }
androidx-compose-ui = { group = "androidx.compose.ui", name = "ui" }
androidx-compose-ui-graphics = { group = "androidx.compose.ui", name = "ui-graphics" }
androidx-compose-ui-tooling-preview = { group = "androidx.compose.ui", name = "ui-tooling-preview" }
androidx-compose-ui-tooling = { group = "androidx.compose.ui", name = "ui-tooling" }
androidx-compose-ui-test-junit4 = { group = "androidx.compose.ui", name = "ui-test-junit4" }
androidx-compose-material3 = { group = "androidx.compose.material3", name = "material3" }
androidx-navigation-compose = { group = "androidx.navigation", name = "navigation-compose", version.ref = "navigation" }
androidx-room-runtime = { group = "androidx.room", name = "room-runtime", version.ref = "room2" }
androidx-room-ktx = { group = "androidx.room", name = "room-ktx", version.ref = "room2" }
androidx-room-compiler = { group = "androidx.room", name = "room-compiler", version.ref = "room2" }
androidx-datastore-preferences = { group = "androidx.datastore", name = "datastore-preferences", version = "1.2.0" } # confirm
androidx-paging-compose = { group = "androidx.paging", name = "paging-compose", version.ref = "paging" }
androidx-camera-camera2 = { group = "androidx.camera", name = "camera-camera2", version.ref = "cameraX" }
androidx-camera-lifecycle = { group = "androidx.camera", name = "camera-lifecycle", version.ref = "cameraX" }
androidx-camera-view = { group = "androidx.camera", name = "camera-view", version.ref = "cameraX" }
androidx-media3-exoplayer = { group = "androidx.media3", name = "media3-exoplayer", version.ref = "media3" }
androidx-media3-ui = { group = "androidx.media3", name = "media3-ui", version.ref = "media3" }
androidx-media3-session = { group = "androidx.media3", name = "media3-session", version.ref = "media3" }
androidx-work-runtime-ktx = { group = "androidx.work", name = "work-runtime-ktx", version.ref = "workManager" }
retrofit = { group = "com.squareup.retrofit2", name = "retrofit", version.ref = "retrofit" }
retrofit-kotlinx-serialization = { group = "com.squareup.retrofit2", name = "converter-kotlinx-serialization", version.ref = "retrofit" }
okhttp = { group = "com.squareup.okhttp3", name = "okhttp", version.ref = "okhttp" }
okhttp-logging = { group = "com.squareup.okhttp3", name = "logging-interceptor", version.ref = "okhttp" }
ktor-client-core = { group = "io.ktor", name = "ktor-client-core", version.ref = "ktor" }
ktor-client-okhttp = { group = "io.ktor", name = "ktor-client-okhttp", version.ref = "ktor" }
kotlinx-coroutines-android = { group = "org.jetbrains.kotlinx", name = "kotlinx-coroutines-android", version.ref = "coroutines" }
kotlinx-serialization-json = { group = "org.jetbrains.kotlinx", name = "kotlinx-serialization-json", version.ref = "serialization" }
coil-compose = { group = "io.coil-kt.coil3", name = "coil-compose", version.ref = "coil" }
coil-network-okhttp = { group = "io.coil-kt.coil3", name = "coil-network-okhttp", version.ref = "coil" }
play-billing = { group = "com.android.billingclient", name = "billing-ktx", version.ref = "billing" }
play-games-v2 = { group = "com.google.android.gms", name = "play-services-games-v2", version.ref = "gamesV2" }
junit = { group = "junit", name = "junit", version = "4.13.2" }
truth = { group = "com.google.truth", name = "truth", version = "1.4.4" } # confirm
turbine = { group = "app.cash.turbine", name = "turbine", version = "1.2.1" } # confirm
mockk = { group = "io.mockk", name = "mockk", version = "1.14.0" } # confirm
leakcanary = { group = "com.squareup.leakcanary", name = "leakcanary-android", version = "2.14" } # confirm

[plugins]
android-application = { id = "com.android.application", version.ref = "agp" }
android-library = { id = "com.android.library", version.ref = "agp" }
kotlin-android = { id = "org.jetbrains.kotlin.android", version.ref = "kotlin" }
kotlin-compose = { id = "org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose", version.ref = "kotlin" }
kotlin-serialization = { id = "org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.serialization", version.ref = "kotlin" }
ksp = { id = "com.google.devtools.ksp", version.ref = "ksp" }
# hilt = { id = "com.google.dagger.hilt.android", version.ref = "hilt" }
```

Room 3 alternative (replace the three Room 2 entries — never both):

```toml
androidx-room3-runtime = { group = "androidx.room3", name = "room-runtime", version.ref = "room3" }
```

## Root `build.gradle.kts`

```kotlin
plugins {
    alias(libs.plugins.android.application) apply false
    alias(libs.plugins.android.library) apply false
    alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.android) apply false
    alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.compose) apply false
    alias(libs.plugins.ksp) apply false
}
```

## App module `build.gradle.kts`

```kotlin
plugins {
    alias(libs.plugins.android.application)
    alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.android)
    alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.compose)   // Kotlin 2.x: this replaces kotlinCompilerExtensionVersion
    alias(libs.plugins.ksp)
}

android {
    namespace = "com.example.app"
    compileSdk = 36        // use 37 only when the chosen Compose BOM requires it

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId = "com.example.app"
        minSdk = 24
        targetSdk = 36     // required for Play since Aug 31, 2026
        versionCode = 1
        versionName = "1.0"
        testInstrumentationRunner = "androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner"
    }

    buildTypes {
        release {
            isMinifyEnabled = true
            isShrinkResources = true
            proguardFiles(getDefaultProguardFile("proguard-android-optimize.txt"), "proguard-rules.pro")
            // signing via Play App Signing; upload key configured outside the repo
        }
    }
    buildFeatures { compose = true }
    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
        targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    }
}

kotlin { jvmToolchain(17) }

dependencies {
    implementation(libs.androidx.core.ktx)
    implementation(libs.androidx.lifecycle.runtime.ktx)
    implementation(libs.androidx.activity.compose)
    implementation(platform(libs.androidx.compose.bom))
    implementation(libs.androidx.compose.ui)
    implementation(libs.androidx.compose.ui.graphics)
    implementation(libs.androidx.compose.ui.tooling.preview)
    implementation(libs.androidx.compose.material3)
    implementation(libs.androidx.navigation.compose)
    implementation(libs.kotlinx.coroutines.android)
    debugImplementation(libs.androidx.compose.ui.tooling)
    debugImplementation(libs.leakcanary)
    testImplementation(libs.junit)
    testImplementation(libs.turbine)
}
```

## `gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties`

```properties
distributionUrl=https\://services.gradle.org/distributions/gradle-9.6-bin.zip
```

## Settings `settings.gradle.kts` repositories

```kotlin
pluginManagement { repositories { google(); mavenCentral(); gradlePluginPortal() } }
dependencyResolutionManagement {
    repositoriesMode.set(RepositoriesMode.FAIL_ON_PROJECT_REPOS)
    repositories { google(); mavenCentral() }
}
```

Related: [`../docs/03-project-setup-gradle-and-tooling.md`](../docs/03-project-setup-gradle-and-tooling.md),
[`library-directory.md`](library-directory.md).

<!-- ===== reference/library-directory.md ===== -->

# Reference — Library Directory

The libraries an Android Kotlin developer actually meets, grouped by
job. Versions are the November 2026 snapshot — **re-check Maven
Central / Google Maven before pinning**; see
[`gradle-and-versions-cheatsheet.md`](gradle-and-versions-cheatsheet.md).
Licenses: Apache-2.0 unless noted.

## Dependency injection

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hilt | Standard DI for Android, built on Dagger; compile-time graph | `com.google.dagger:hilt-android` (+ KSP compiler) — exact latest **Unverified**, check Google Maven | Use for most multi-module / tested apps. Avoid for tiny single-screen apps — manual DI is less machinery. |
| Dagger | The DI engine under Hilt | `com.google.dagger:dagger` | Use directly only when Hilt's Android assumptions do not fit. |
| Koin | Runtime (service-locator style) DI, popular in KMP | `io.insert-koin:koin-android` | Use for KMP shared code or when compile-time DI is unwanted. Avoid when you need compile-time graph errors. |

## Persistence & data

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| Room 2 | SQLite ORM, Android-only line | `androidx.room:room-runtime` / `room-ktx` (2.x) | Use in existing Room 2 projects. KAPT or KSP. |
| Room 3 | SQLite ORM, KMP-ready line | `androidx.room3:room-runtime` (3.x) | Use for new projects that need shared/KMP database code. **Different artifacts and setup from Room 2 — never mix.** |
| DataStore | Key-value / typed settings storage (Preferences + Proto) | `androidx.datastore:datastore-preferences` / `datastore` | Use instead of SharedPreferences for all new settings storage. |
| SQLDelight | Type-safe SQL, KMP-first (Square) | `app.cash.sqldelight:android-driver` (+ runtime) | Use when the database is shared with iOS/Desktop via KMP. |
| Paging 3 | Paged loading from DB/network (snapshot 3.5.3) | `androidx.paging:paging-runtime-ktx` / `paging-compose` | Use for long scrolling lists backed by Room or an API. Avoid for small in-memory lists. |
| WorkManager | Deferrable, guaranteed background work | `androidx.work:work-runtime-ktx` | The default for sync, uploads, cleanup. Not for exact-time or immediate work. |

## UI

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| Compose BOM | Version-aligns all Compose libraries (snapshot BOM 2026.09.00) | `androidx.compose:compose-bom` (platform) | Always import the BOM; pin individual Compose versions only to override deliberately. |
| Compose UI / Material 3 | The Compose toolkit + Material 3 components | `androidx.compose.ui:ui`, `androidx.compose.material3:material3` (via BOM) | Default UI stack for new apps. |
| Navigation 2 | Fragment/Compose navigation, stable line | `androidx.navigation:navigation-compose` (2.x) | Safe default for Compose navigation today. |
| Navigation 3 | New navigation library (scene/back-stack model) | `androidx.navigation3:*` | Adopt for new projects after checking its current stability status in the release notes. |
| Coil 3 | Kotlin-first image loading (snapshot: Coil 3 line) | `io.coil-kt.coil3:coil-compose`, `io.coil-kt.coil3:coil-network-okhttp` | Default image loader for Compose. **Coil 3 coordinates differ from Coil 2** (`io.coil-kt:coil-compose`). |
| Glide | Mature image loading, strong for Views/GIFs (snapshot: 5.x line) | `com.github.bumptech.glide:glide` (+ `compose` integration) | Use in View-based apps or where its disk-cache behavior is needed. |
| CameraX | Camera lifecycle + use cases (snapshot 1.6.2) | `androidx.camera:camera-camera2`, `camera-lifecycle`, `camera-view` | The default camera stack. Avoid raw Camera2 unless CameraX cannot do the job. |
| Media3 | Playback (ExoPlayer successor), UI, session (snapshot 1.11.1) | `androidx.media3:media3-exoplayer`, `media3-ui`, `media3-session` | The only playback stack for new code. **Replaces standalone ExoPlayer 2.** |

## Networking & serialization

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| Retrofit 3 | Type-safe REST client over OkHttp (snapshot 3.0.0) | `com.squareup.retrofit2:retrofit` | Default REST client for Android-only apps. |
| OkHttp 5 | HTTP client, interceptors, caching (snapshot 5.5.1) | `com.squareup.okhttp3:okhttp` | Comes with Retrofit; also used directly. |
| Ktor Client | Kotlin-first HTTP client, KMP-ready (3.x line) | `io.ktor:ktor-client-core` (+ engine, e.g. `ktor-client-okhttp` / `ktor-client-android`) | Use when networking code is shared in KMP. |
| kotlinx.serialization | Kotlin-first JSON (and more) serialization | `org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-serialization-json` (+ compiler plugin) | Default serializer with Retrofit/Ktor converters. Moshi/Gson only for existing codebases. |

## Concurrency

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| kotlinx.coroutines | Structured concurrency + Flow (snapshot 1.11.0) | `org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-coroutines-android` | Foundation of all modern Android async code. Never `GlobalScope`. |
| Turbine | Flow testing assertions | `app.cash.turbine:turbine` | Use in unit tests for any non-trivial Flow. |

## Testing

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| JUnit / Truth | Test runner / fluent assertions | `junit:junit`, `com.google.truth:truth` | Standard unit-test pair. |
| MockK | Kotlin mocking | `io.mockk:mockk` | Use for mocks; prefer fakes for repositories/data sources. |
| Robolectric | Android framework tests on the JVM | `org.robolectric:robolectric` | Use for fast framework-dependent tests; keep some real-device tests too. |
| Roborazzi | Screenshot testing (Robolectric-based) | `io.github.takahirom.roborazzi:roborazzi` | Use for Compose/View screenshot regression tests. |
| LeakCanary | Memory-leak detection in debug builds (Square) | `com.squareup.leakcanary:leakcanary-android` | Debug builds only; never ship it in release. |
| Compose Test / Espresso | UI & instrumented tests | `androidx.compose.ui:ui-test-junit4` (via BOM), `androidx.test.espresso:*` | Compose tests for Compose UI; Espresso for View/interop flows. |
| Macrobenchmark / Baseline Profiles | Startup & scroll performance measurement | `androidx.benchmark:benchmark-macro-junit4`, `androidx.profileinstaller:profileinstaller` | Use before release on any performance-sensitive app. |

## Games

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates / source | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| libGDX | Cross-platform game framework (Java/Kotlin) | `com.badlogicgames.gdx:gdx` (+ `gdx-backend-android`) | Default engine route for 2D/3D Kotlin games. |
| KTX | Idiomatic Kotlin extensions for libGDX | `io.github.libktx:*` modules | Use with libGDX in Kotlin — do not write raw libGDX Kotlin without it. |
| KorGE | Kotlin-native 2D engine, multiplatform | `com.soywiz.korge:korge` — current version **Unverified** | Use for KMP-style games; verify version on Maven Central first. |
| Godot + godot-kotlin/jvm | Full engine with Kotlin/JVM scripting | Engine download + `org.godotfoundation.godot-kotlin:*` (check repo) | Use when the team/project is already on Godot. |
| Play Games Services v2 | Sign-in, achievements, leaderboards, saves | `com.google.android.gms:play-services-games-v2` | The only PGS line for new games. **Never PGS v1.** |
| AGDK | Native game libraries (GameActivity, frame pacing) | Via NDK / prefab: `com.google.android.games:*` where published | Use for C/C++ or hybrid native games. |

## Backend

| Library | Purpose | Coordinates | When to use / avoid |
|---|---|---|---|
| Firebase BoM | Aligns Firebase libraries (Auth, Firestore, FCM, Analytics…) | `com.google.firebase:firebase-bom` — exact version **Unverified**, check firebase.google.com | Use when the backend is Firebase; pin via the BoM only. |

## Avoid list — do not start new code on these

| Thing | Why not | Use instead |
|---|---|---|
| Picasso | Maintenance-era image loader | Coil 3 (Compose) / Glide (Views) |
| Standalone ExoPlayer 2 (`com.google.android.exoplayer2`) | Superseded; security/feature fixes land in Media3 | Media3 (`androidx.media3`) |
| AsyncTask | Deprecated since API 30; leaks and lifecycle bugs | Coroutines / WorkManager |
| PGS v1 (legacy Games / GoogleSignIn APIs) | Legacy sign-in path, superseded by PGS v2 | Play Games Services v2 |
| SharedPreferences for new storage | Synchronous, no corruption safety, no Flow | DataStore |
| KAPT for new code | Slower, superseded by KSP | KSP |
| `kotlinCompilerExtensionVersion` | Kotlin 1.x Compose setup; wrong on Kotlin 2.x | Compose compiler Gradle plugin |

<!-- ===== reference/permissions-table.md ===== -->

# Reference — Permissions Table

Runtime ("dangerous") permissions, the API levels that changed them,
and the foreground service (FGS) types. Snapshot checked November
2026; the binding list is
https://developer.android.com/reference/android/Manifest.permission
and the FGS types page on developer.android.com.

## Dangerous permissions by area

| Permission | Added / changed | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` | Runtime since API 33 | Notifications are opt-in on Android 13+. Ask after the user sees value, not at first launch. |
| `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES` / `READ_MEDIA_VIDEO` / `READ_MEDIA_AUDIO` | API 33 | Replace `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` for media on Android 13+. Prefer the **photo picker** (no permission at all) for user-selected media. |
| `READ_MEDIA_VISUAL_STORIES` | API 35-era addition — verify on the permission reference | Granular media categories keep splitting; check before requesting broad media access. |
| `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` | Max SDK 32 in practice | Legacy. On API 33+ use the media permissions or the picker; scoped storage is the model since API 29/30. |
| `WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` | Legacy | No effect on modern targets — shared storage writes go through MediaStore / app-specific dirs / SAF. |
| `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` / `ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION` | Runtime since API 23 | Request coarse first where it suffices; the system offers approximate location. |
| `ACCESS_BACKGROUND_LOCATION` | API 29+ | Separate, later request; requires a Play Console declaration and a clear user benefit. Never bundle with the foreground request. |
| `CAMERA` | Runtime since API 23 | Declare `android.hardware.camera` features as `required=false` where the camera is optional. |
| `RECORD_AUDIO` | Runtime since API 23 | Same pattern as camera. |
| `BODY_SENSORS` / `BODY_SENSORS_BACKGROUND` | Split in API 36-era health changes — verify current form | Health/body data moved toward Health Connect permissions; check the current guidance before requesting. |
| `ACTIVITY_RECOGNITION` | API 29+ | Runtime permission for step/activity detection. |
| `BLUETOOTH_SCAN` / `BLUETOOTH_CONNECT` / `BLUETOOTH_ADVERTISE` | API 31 | Replace the old `BLUETOOTH`/`BLUETOOTH_ADMIN` + location pairing for BLE on Android 12+. `neverForLocation` flag available on scan. |
| `NEARBY_WIFI_DEVICES` | API 33 | For Wi-Fi peer discovery without location. |
| `SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM` | API 31+ (special) | Not a normal runtime grant on API 33+ targets — user grants in Settings; Play declaration needed for most uses. |
| `USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT` | Restricted API 34+ | Limited to calling/alarm-style apps; Play declaration required. |
| `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES` | Special | Outside-Play installs; heavily policy-restricted. Games/apps on Play should not need it. |

## Normal / signature highlights

- `INTERNET` and `ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE` are normal (install-time),
  declared in the manifest, no runtime prompt.
- `VIBRATE`, `WAKE_LOCK`, `FOREGROUND_SERVICE` (+ per-type
  permissions below) are normal but FGS use is policy-checked.

## Foreground service types (declare type + permission)

Since API 34 every foreground service declares a type in the manifest
(`android:foregroundServiceType`) and the matching permission; Play
also requires an FGS declaration in Play Console.

| Type | Permission | Typical use |
|---|---|---|
| `dataSync` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC` | Upload/download/sync started by the user or app logic |
| `mediaPlayback` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK` | Media3 playback sessions |
| `mediaProjection` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PROJECTION` | Screen capture/sharing |
| `phoneCall` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_PHONE_CALL` | Call management |
| `location` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_LOCATION` | Navigation / tracking with a visible notification |
| `connectedDevice` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CONNECTED_DEVICE` | Bluetooth/USB companion work |
| `health` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_HEALTH` | Sensor/health recording |
| `remoteMessaging` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_REMOTE_MESSAGING` | Message relay |
| `microphone` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MICROPHONE` | Foreground recording (needs `RECORD_AUDIO` granted first) |
| `camera` | `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CAMERA` | Foreground camera use (needs `CAMERA` granted first) |
| `systemExempted` / `specialUse` | Restricted | Only for qualifying system roles / declared special use — expect Play scrutiny |

API 35+ adds time limits to some types (notably `dataSync` media
processing windows) — check the FGS changes page for your targetSdk.

## Rules of thumb

1. Prefer **no-permission alternatives** first: photo picker (media),
   app-specific storage (files), approximate location, exact-alarm
   alternatives (WorkManager / inexact alarms).
2. Request **in context**, one purpose at a time, with an in-app
   rationale shown before the system dialog.
3. Handle **denial and "don't ask again"** gracefully — the feature
   degrades, the app keeps working.
4. Every sensitive permission in the manifest must be justified in
   the Play data safety form and, where required, a permissions
   declaration form.

Related: [`../docs/15-permissions-security-and-privacy.md`](../docs/15-permissions-security-and-privacy.md),
[`../docs/14-background-work-and-foreground-services.md`](../docs/14-background-work-and-foreground-services.md).

<!-- ===== reference/play-requirements-and-deadlines.md ===== -->

# Reference — Play Requirements and Deadlines

The Google Play requirements that gate whether a submission is even
accepted. Snapshot checked November 2026. The binding text is always
the Play Console help / policy pages — re-check before every release
([`../SOURCES.md`](../SOURCES.md)).

## Target API timeline

| Requirement | Date | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Target API 36 for new apps and app updates | **August 31, 2026** | New apps and updates must target API 36 (Android 16) or higher. Existing apps below the required target face discoverability/update restrictions per the Play target API policy. |
| Target API 35 era | Aug 31, 2025 (past) | The previous step (API 35, Android 15) — historical, superseded by the API 36 step above. |
| Wear OS / TV / Auto targets | Per-form-factor dates in Play Console help | Form-factor apps have their own target API steps — check the requirement page for each form factor you ship. |

Practical rule: set `targetSdk = 36` now, `compileSdk = 36` (37 only
where the newest Compose features require it), and start the next
target bump early — behavior changes (edge-to-edge, permission and
background limits, large-screen rules) are the real work, not the
number.

## Billing

| Requirement | Detail |
|---|---|
| Billing Library 8+ | Apps using Play Billing must integrate **Billing Library 8 or newer**; submissions/updates on older versions are rejected under the current Play billing requirements. Migrate per [`../docs/20-monetization-billing-and-ads.md`](../docs/20-monetization-billing-and-ads.md). |
| Subscriptions & pricing rules | Acknowledge purchases within 3 days or they auto-refund; test with license testers; declare offers/pricing in Play Console to match in-app claims. |

## 16 KB page size

| Milestone | Date | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Requirement announced / tooling available | 2025 | Apps with native code (`.so` libraries) must support 16 KB memory page sizes on Android 15+ devices. |
| Play enforcement step-up | **February 1, 2027** | Play enforcement tightens: new apps and updates that ship native code not aligned for 16 KB pages face rejection. Rebuild native libraries (NDK r27+ defaults / explicit `-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384`) and check every bundled `.so`, including third-party SDKs and game engines. |

Check with: `llvm-readelf -lW lib.so | grep LOAD` (alignment `0x4000`)
or the APK Analyzer 16 KB check in Android Studio. This applies to
libGDX/native/AGDK games and any app bundling native libraries.

## App Bundle and signing

| Requirement | Detail |
|---|---|
| Android App Bundle (AAB) | Required for new apps since August 2021; updates ship as AABs. Play generates the per-device APKs. |
| Play App Signing | Required for new apps: Google holds the final signing key; you sign uploads with your upload key. Keep the upload key + passwords in a secret store, never in the repo. |
| 64-bit native code | Native code must ship 64-bit (`arm64-v8a`, `x86_64`) variants. |

## Data safety and privacy

| Requirement | Detail |
|---|---|
| Data safety section | Every app must complete the Play Console data safety form: data collected, shared, purposes, encryption in transit, deletion path. It must match the privacy policy and the app's actual behavior. |
| Privacy policy | Required for apps that access personal/sensitive data (and all apps in several categories); hosted at a stable public URL. |
| Account deletion | Apps that let users create accounts must offer in-app account deletion and a web deletion link declared in Play Console. |
| Permissions declarations | Sensitive permissions (location background, health, photos/video, notifications, exact alarm, etc.) require Play Console declaration forms and a demo video in some cases — see [`permissions-table.md`](permissions-table.md). |
| Families / Designed for Families | Apps targeting children carry extra policy (ads SDK certification, content, data) — check before declaring a child target audience. |

## Release mechanics worth planning around

- **Review times vary** — new developer accounts and new apps can
  face longer review and a closed-testing requirement (personal
  accounts created after Nov 2023 need 12+ testers for 14 days of
  closed testing before production access — verify current terms in
  Play Console help).
- **Staged rollout** is the default safe path for updates: small
  percentage, watch crashes/ANRs in Android vitals, then expand.
- **Android vitals thresholds** (bad behavior): excessive crash/ANR
  rates hurt discoverability — monitor after every rollout step.

Related: [`../docs/19-build-signing-release-and-google-play.md`](../docs/19-build-signing-release-and-google-play.md),
[`../checklists/play-submission-checklist.md`](../checklists/play-submission-checklist.md).

<!-- ===== checklists/game-release-checklist.md ===== -->

# Game Release Checklist

Run in addition to [`pre-release-checklist.md`](pre-release-checklist.md)
and [`play-submission-checklist.md`](play-submission-checklist.md) for
any game, on any route ([`../reference/game-route-comparison.md`](../reference/game-route-comparison.md)).

## Game services and identity

- [ ] Play Games Services **v2** integrated (automatic sign-in works;
      no PGS v1 code paths remain) —
      [`../docs/27-games-services-play-games-and-play-games-on-pc.md`](../docs/27-games-services-play-games-and-play-games-on-pc.md).
- [ ] Play Console game project linked; achievements/leaderboards in
      the build are published (not left in draft) and vice versa.
- [ ] Saved games / cloud save conflict flow tested (two devices or
      reinstall): newest-progress wins, no silent overwrite.
- [ ] Sign-in declined path works — the game is fully playable signed
      out, with services features degrading cleanly.

## Performance and devices

- [ ] Frame budget held on a mid-range device at targetSdk: stable
      frame pacing, no per-frame allocation spikes in the hot loop —
      [`../docs/28-games-performance-input-audio-and-saves.md`](../docs/28-games-performance-input-audio-and-saves.md).
- [ ] Thermal pass: 20+ minute session without sustained throttling
      collapse; quality scaling (if any) engages.
- [ ] Low-end device at minSdk tested; high-refresh (90/120 Hz)
      device tested — game speed is frame-rate independent.
- [ ] Native builds: all `.so` files 64-bit and 16 KB page-aligned;
      symbols archived for crash symbolication.
- [ ] Download size checked (bundletool); asset delivery (Play Asset
      Delivery) used where the game exceeds comfortable base size.

## Input, display, audio

- [ ] Touch, and (where claimed) game controller, keyboard/mouse all
      tested; controller disconnect mid-game handled.
- [ ] Foldable posture changes, resize, and aspect ratios from
      4:3 to ultra-wide render without stretched or cut-off gameplay.
- [ ] Google Play Games on PC checks done if opting in: resizable
      window, keyboard/mouse, no touch-only blockers.
- [ ] Audio: focus loss (call/notification) pauses or ducks; silent
      mode and Bluetooth latency cases sane; no audio after the app
      is backgrounded.

## Monetization and policy

- [ ] Billing 8+ flows tested with license testers (purchase,
      restore, refund/revoke handling); prices in-game match Play
      Console exactly.
- [ ] No loot-box / gacha mechanics without the required odds
      disclosure where policy demands it; ratings questionnaire
      answers reflect the game's actual content and monetization.
- [ ] Offline play works where promised; server-dependent features
      fail with a clear message, not a hang.
- [ ] Anything not run on a real device is recorded as
      **Unverified** in the release notes.

<!-- ===== checklists/new-project-checklist.md ===== -->

# New Project Checklist

Run at project start, before feature work. Targets:
[`../docs/02-android-platform-and-versions.md`](../docs/02-android-platform-and-versions.md),
[`../docs/03-project-setup-gradle-and-tooling.md`](../docs/03-project-setup-gradle-and-tooling.md).

## Target first (`AGENTS.md` rule 2)

- [ ] Target stated in writing: app or game; for a game, the route
      (Compose Canvas / libGDX / KorGE / Godot / native AGDK —
      [`../reference/game-route-comparison.md`](../reference/game-route-comparison.md)).
- [ ] compileSdk / targetSdk / minSdk chosen and recorded
      (snapshot: compile 36, target 36, min 24–26 unless the audience
      says otherwise).
- [ ] Form factors in scope named (phone / tablet / foldable / Wear /
      TV / Auto) — [`../docs/21-form-factors-wear-tv-auto-foldables.md`](../docs/21-form-factors-wear-tv-auto-foldables.md).

## Project setup

- [ ] Created with the current Android Studio (snapshot: Rabbit 2,
      2026.2.2) or the matching templates in [`../templates/`](../templates/).
- [ ] Kotlin 2.x with the **Compose compiler plugin** applied; no
      `kotlinCompilerExtensionVersion` anywhere.
- [ ] Gradle wrapper at 9.6, AGP 9.5 line, JDK 17 toolchain.
- [ ] Versions centralized in `gradle/libs.versions.toml`; Compose
      imported via the BOM (2026.09.00 snapshot) — every version
      re-checked on Maven Central / Google Maven at setup time.
- [ ] KSP (not KAPT) configured for Room/Hilt/codegen.
- [ ] Package name / `applicationId` chosen (reverse-domain, permanent
      once published) and `namespace` set per module.
- [ ] Clean `./gradlew assembleDebug` build runs on an emulator at
      targetSdk and one at minSdk.

## Structure

- [ ] Architecture shape picked (single module vs modularized;
      UI / domain / data layers) — [`../docs/08-architecture-patterns.md`](../docs/08-architecture-patterns.md).
- [ ] DI approach picked (manual / Hilt / Koin) —
      [`../docs/09-dependency-injection.md`](../docs/09-dependency-injection.md).
- [ ] Git repo initialized; `.gitignore` covers `local.properties`,
      keystores, `*.jks`, build output; no secrets committed
      (`AGENTS.md` rule 9).
- [ ] License file and README skeleton in place.

## Early decisions (expensive to change later)

- [ ] Persistence plan: Room 2 **or** Room 3 (never both), DataStore
      for settings — [`../docs/11-data-persistence-room-datastore-files.md`](../docs/11-data-persistence-room-datastore-files.md).
- [ ] Navigation approach: Navigation 2 (stable) or Navigation 3.
- [ ] Monetization plan noted (Billing 8+ if purchases exist) and,
      for games, PGS v2 planned from the start.
- [ ] Baseline Profile + Macrobenchmark modules planned for
      performance-sensitive apps — [`../docs/17-performance-profiling-and-optimization.md`](../docs/17-performance-profiling-and-optimization.md).


---

<!-- ===== checklists/performance-audit-checklist.md ===== -->

# Performance Audit Checklist

Run periodically and before any performance-sensitive release.
Method: [`../docs/17-performance-profiling-and-optimization.md`](../docs/17-performance-profiling-and-optimization.md);
games also use [`../docs/28-games-performance-input-audio-and-saves.md`](../docs/28-games-performance-input-audio-and-saves.md).

## Startup

- [ ] Cold start measured with Macrobenchmark (median, not best run)
      and compared with the previous release — regression >10% is a
      release blocker until explained.
- [ ] Baseline Profile current (regenerated after UI/navigation
      changes) and shipped via ProfileInstaller.
- [ ] No blocking I/O, database, or network work on the main thread
      during startup; Application.onCreate is thin.
- [ ] Startup trace (Perfetto / Android Studio profiler) reviewed:
      no surprise library initializers doing heavy work.

## Frame rendering

- [ ] Main lists scroll without jank: stable item keys, no heavy work
      in item composables, images sized to their targets (Coil/Glide).
- [ ] Recomposition counts checked on hot screens (Layout Inspector /
      Compose tooling); state hoisted, unstable parameters fixed.
- [ ] `dumpsys gfxinfo framestats` or Macrobenchmark frame timing on
      the top flows shows p90 frame time inside the refresh budget.
- [ ] Games: frame pacing library / swapchain behavior verified;
      allocation-free hot loop confirmed in the profiler.

## Memory

- [ ] LeakCanary clean on the main flows; heap dump reviewed after a
      full user journey.
- [ ] Bitmap/image memory bounded (no full-resolution decode for
      thumbnails); caches have explicit size limits.
- [ ] Memory does not grow across repeated navigation to the same
      screens (leaked collectors / unregistered listeners).

## Network, battery, storage

- [ ] Requests batched/cached where possible; OkHttp cache and
      retry/backoff sane; no polling loops (WorkManager/FCM instead).
- [ ] Battery Historian / vitals: no excessive wake locks; FGS use
      justified and typed.
- [ ] Database queries indexed and explained (`EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN`
      on the hot ones); Paging used for unbounded lists.
- [ ] App size trend checked per release (apkanalyzer compare);
      growth explained in the release notes when it jumps.


---

<!-- ===== checklists/play-submission-checklist.md ===== -->

# Play Submission Checklist

Run in Play Console for each production submission. Requirements
detail: [`../reference/play-requirements-and-deadlines.md`](../reference/play-requirements-and-deadlines.md).

## Policy gates

- [ ] App targets API 36 (required for new apps/updates since
      August 31, 2026); AAB uploaded (not APK).
- [ ] Billing Library 8+ integrated if the app sells digital goods;
      no external payment links for digital goods unless a current
      Play program explicitly allows them.
- [ ] Data safety section completed and accurate against the actual
      build (SDKs included — audit third-party SDK data practices).
- [ ] Privacy policy URL live and matching the data safety answers.
- [ ] Account deletion: in-app path works; web deletion URL declared
      (for apps with account creation).
- [ ] Permissions declarations submitted for every sensitive
      permission / FGS type the build declares
      ([`../reference/permissions-table.md`](../reference/permissions-table.md)).
- [ ] Content rating questionnaire completed; target audience and
      Families answers accurate.
- [ ] Ads declaration accurate (contains ads / no ads); ad SDKs, if
      any, are Families-certified when children are in the audience.

## Store listing

- [ ] Title, short and full descriptions factual; no keyword stuffing,
      no claims the build does not support.
- [ ] Screenshots are real captures of the current build on the
      declared form factors; feature graphic current.
- [ ] Release notes contain only this version's changes, in plain
      language.
- [ ] Contact email and developer details current.

## Rollout

- [ ] Closed/internal testing pass completed first (and the
      12-tester / 14-day closed test satisfied where the account
      requires it — verify current terms in Play Console help).
- [ ] Staged rollout percentage chosen (small first step); halt
      criteria decided before starting.
- [ ] Android vitals watched during rollout (crash/ANR vs bad-behavior
      thresholds); mapping file uploaded/kept for symbolication.
- [ ] Rollback plan known: halt rollout, ship a fixed version code —
      a published version code can never be reused.


---

<!-- ===== checklists/pre-release-checklist.md ===== -->

# Pre-Release Checklist

Run on the release candidate before uploading anything.
Release process: [`../docs/19-build-signing-release-and-google-play.md`](../docs/19-build-signing-release-and-google-play.md).

## Build

- [ ] Version code incremented; version name matches the release notes.
- [ ] `./gradlew bundleRelease` produces the AAB from a clean checkout
      of the release commit (not a dirty working tree).
- [ ] Release build has R8 minification + resource shrinking on;
      mapping file (`mapping.txt`) archived for crash deobfuscation.
- [ ] Baseline Profile generated and included; startup checked with
      Macrobenchmark against the previous release.
- [ ] App size reviewed with apkanalyzer / bundletool
      ([`../reference/adb-and-tooling-commands.md`](../reference/adb-and-tooling-commands.md));
      no accidental test assets, debug libraries, or duplicate media.
- [ ] Native libraries (if any) are 64-bit and 16 KB page-aligned
      ([`../reference/play-requirements-and-deadlines.md`](../reference/play-requirements-and-deadlines.md)).

## Code and quality

- [ ] Full test suite green: unit, Flow (Turbine), repository/fakes,
      Compose UI tests; screenshot tests (Roborazzi) updated and reviewed.
- [ ] Lint clean on the release variant (no new errors; baseline not
      grown without a recorded reason).
- [ ] LeakCanary shows no leaks on the main flows (debug build pass).
- [ ] No debug logging of personal data, no test endpoints, no
      placeholder keys in the release build.
- [ ] Offline / airplane-mode pass: core flows degrade gracefully;
      WorkManager sync resumes.

## Behavior

- [ ] Fresh install, upgrade-from-previous-release, and
      clear-data flows all tested on a real device or emulator.
- [ ] Process death + rotation + dark theme + font scale 1.3x pass on
      the top screens.
- [ ] Permissions: every prompt appears in context; denial paths work
      ([`../reference/permissions-table.md`](../reference/permissions-table.md)).
- [ ] Billing (if present): license-tester purchase, restore,
      pending, and subscription states verified on a test track.
- [ ] Anything not actually run on a device/emulator is recorded as
      **Unverified** in the release notes — not silently implied.

## Signing

- [ ] Upload key + passwords pulled from the secret store (not the
      repo); Play App Signing enrollment confirmed in Play Console.
- [ ] AAB signature verified (`jarsigner -verify` / Play Console
      upload check) before the rollout starts.


---

<!-- ===== checklists/security-and-privacy-checklist.md ===== -->

# Security and Privacy Checklist

Run before first release and whenever data handling changes.
Detail: [`../docs/15-permissions-security-and-privacy.md`](../docs/15-permissions-security-and-privacy.md).

## Secrets and keys

- [ ] No API keys, passwords, tokens, keystores, or service-account
      files in the repo or the git history (`AGENTS.md` rule 9).
- [ ] Client-embedded keys (where unavoidable) are restricted in
      their provider console (package name + signing SHA) and treated
      as public; anything truly secret lives on a server.
- [ ] Upload key stored in a password manager / secret store with a
      backup owner; Play App Signing recovery path understood.

## Data protection

- [ ] Sensitive data encrypted at rest (EncryptedFile / Keystore-backed
      keys); tokens never in plain SharedPreferences/DataStore.
- [ ] All network traffic is HTTPS; network security config forbids
      cleartext (no `cleartextTrafficPermitted` exceptions without a
      written reason); certificate pinning considered for first-party
      APIs with a rotation plan.
- [ ] WebViews (if any): JavaScript interfaces minimal, file access
      off, only trusted content loaded.
- [ ] Logs contain no personal data, tokens, or request bodies with
      user content — in release builds especially.
- [ ] Backups: `dataExtractionRules` / backup rules exclude tokens and
      sensitive files deliberately, not by accident.

## Permissions and privacy behavior

- [ ] Manifest declares the minimum permissions; every dangerous
      permission is requested in context with a rationale
      ([`../reference/permissions-table.md`](../reference/permissions-table.md)).
- [ ] No permission is used for a purpose different from the one
      shown to the user; background location / special permissions
      only where the feature truly qualifies.
- [ ] Data safety answers match the build: audit analytics/ads SDKs'
      actual collection before answering "no data shared".
- [ ] Privacy policy covers what the app actually does today; consent
      flows (where required) gate the SDKs they describe.
- [ ] User data controls work: export/delete where promised; account
      deletion path tested end to end.

## Platform hardening

- [ ] Components (`exported`) audited: nothing exported without a
      reason; deep links verified (app links with assetlinks.json)
      where claimed.
- [ ] PendingIntents use explicit mutability flags; no implicit
      intent carries sensitive data.
- [ ] Release build: `debuggable=false`, R8 on, no test activities
      or debug receivers reachable.

<!-- ===== templates/README.md ===== -->

# Templates

Copy-paste starting points. Every version below comes from
[`../tools/versions.json`](../tools/versions.json) (verified
November 2026) — re-check against the official release notes before
starting a production project.

| Template | What it is |
|---|---|
| [`compose-app/`](compose-app/) | Minimal single-module Jetpack Compose app: Kotlin 2.4.21, AGP 9.5, compileSdk 37, targetSdk 36, minSdk 24, Material 3, version catalog included. Scaffold it with [`../tools/new-project.sh`](../tools/new-project.sh) / [`new-project.ps1`](../tools/new-project.ps1). |
| [`github-workflows/android-ci.yml`](github-workflows/android-ci.yml) | GitHub Actions CI: JDK 17, Gradle cache, lint + unit tests + `assembleDebug` on push / pull request. Copy to `.github/workflows/android-ci.yml`. |
| [`github-workflows/release-aab.yml`](github-workflows/release-aab.yml) | Manual-dispatch release workflow: builds `bundleRelease` and uploads the AAB as a workflow artifact. Signing uses CI secrets — see the comments in the file; no secret is ever printed. Copy to `.github/workflows/release-aab.yml`. |

## Using `compose-app`

1. Run the scaffolder (recommended — it renames the package for you):

   ```bash
   # bash / zsh / Git Bash
   tools/new-project.sh MyApp com.example.myapp
   ```

   ```powershell
   # PowerShell
   tools\new-project.ps1 -ProjectName MyApp -PackageId com.example.myapp
   ```

2. Or copy `compose-app/` by hand and replace every `com.example.app`
   and `ComposeApp` occurrence with your own values.
3. Open the folder in Android Studio (Rabbit 2 2026.2.2 or newer),
   let Gradle sync, and run the `app` configuration.
4. Validate the result any time:

   ```bash
   python3 tools/check-project.py path/to/MyApp
   ```

<!-- ===== examples/README.md ===== -->

# Examples

Small, focused teaching examples. Each folder isolates one pattern so
it can be read in a few minutes and copied into a real project.

> **Disclaimer (applies to every example):** each example is a teaching
> excerpt, not a full Gradle project — files are trimmed to the pattern
> being taught and will not compile standalone without a host project.
> The full, buildable skeleton is [`../templates/compose-app/`](../templates/compose-app/);
> drop the example's files into a copy of that template (matching the
> package name) to run them. Versions follow
> [`../tools/versions.json`](../tools/versions.json) (verified
> November 2026).

| Example | Pattern |
|---|---|
| [`01-hello-compose/`](01-hello-compose/) | Smallest Compose screen: `ComponentActivity`, `setContent`, Material 3, a `@Preview`. Start here. |
| [`02-counter-mvvm-flow/`](02-counter-mvvm-flow/) | MVVM + unidirectional data flow: `StateFlow` in the ViewModel, stateless composable, events flow up. |
| [`03-room-notes/`](03-room-notes/) | Local persistence: Room 2.x entity / DAO / repository with a `Flow` query. Includes the Room 3 package-change note. |
| [`04-canvas-game-loop/`](04-canvas-game-loop/) | Game basics in pure Compose: `Canvas`, a `withFrameNanos` frame loop, delta-time movement, pause on lifecycle stop. |

<!-- ===== tools/README.md ===== -->

# Tools

Standalone utilities that speed up Android development in Kotlin.
None of them need an emulator or a device.

| Tool | What it does |
|---|---|
| [`new-project.sh`](new-project.sh) / [`new-project.ps1`](new-project.ps1) | Scaffolds a new Compose app from [`../templates/compose-app/`](../templates/compose-app/) into a ready-to-open folder: substitutes the project name and package id, renames the package folders. |
| [`check-project.py`](check-project.py) | Static validator for an Android project folder: settings/build files exist, version catalog present, manifest sanity, `applicationId` / `targetSdk` warnings, hardcoded API-key scan. Python 3, standard library only. |
| [`versions.json`](versions.json) | The verified version snapshot (November 2026) from the docs in machine-readable form — Kotlin, AGP, Gradle, Compose BOM, SDK levels, and the key Jetpack libraries. Re-check before production use; see its `verifyBeforeUse` note. |
| [`ide.md`](ide.md) | Android Studio setup notes: recommended settings, what is bundled vs what to install, and a copy-paste `.editorconfig`. |
| [`build-ai-bundle.py`](build-ai-bundle.py) | Regenerates the repo-root `manifest.json` (machine-readable file map + reading order) and `ai-bundle.md` (the whole knowledge base as one file) so AI tools that download the repo — ChatGPT included — can ingest it locally. Run after any content change. |

Usage examples are in each file's header.
